;;; $8000: Level layer 1 object streams (overflow) (LevelData078000) ;;;
;;; $8100: Level object streams (bank 07 overflow) (LevelData078100) ;;;
;;; $A600: Overflow level layer-1 object data (LevelDataSpr07A600) ;;;
;;; $C300: Sprite lists, goal bonus digits (LevelDataSpr07C300) ;;;
;;; $F24E..F24E: Table: effect tile numbers ;;;
;;; $F252: Set score value from height and give points ;;;
;;; $F26C: Table: sprite tweaker $1656 by sprite number (Sprite1656Vals) ;;;
;;; $F335: Table: sprite tweaker $1662 by sprite number (Sprite1662Vals) ;;;
;;; $F3FE: Table: sprite tweaker $166E by sprite number (Sprite166EVals) ;;;
;;; $F4C7: Table: sprite tweaker $167A by sprite number (Sprite167AVals) ;;;
;;; $F590: Table: sprite tweaker $1686 by sprite number (Sprite1686Vals) ;;;
;;; $F659: Table: sprite tweaker $190F by sprite number (Sprite190FVals) ;;;
;;; $F722: Clear per-sprite state tables (ZeroSpriteTables) ;;;
;;; $F78B: Load sprite palette and tweaker bytes (LoadSpriteTables) ;;;
;;; $F7A0: Copy tweaker bytes into sprite slot (LoadTweakerBytes) ;;;
;;; $F7D2: Initialise sprite slot tables (InitSpriteTables) ;;;
;;; $F7DB: Sine table and line-guide path data (CircleCoords) ;;;
;;; $FC90..FFF8: Freespace (filled with $FF) ;;;


;;; $8000: Level layer 1 object streams (overflow) (LevelData078000) ;;;
{
;; Overflow level layer-1 object streams for bank 06 levels (see Layer1Ptrs). Each stream: 5-byte primary header, then 3-byte objects
;; until $FF. Header: screens, level mode, sprite GFX, music, tileset. Object: byte1 [new scr|obj hi|Y], byte2 [obj lo|X], byte3 size/ext #.
;; Extended objects (object number 0) use byte3 as index into PtrsLong0DA10F; names shown are from that table (tileset-dependent entries may differ).
;; Eleven streams start at $8000, $8018, $801E, $802D, $804E, $8060, $807F, $8090, $809F, $80B1 and $80C3 (a vertical level of slopes).
$07:8000                .db $C0,$6F,$49,$04,$05,$6C,$31,$03 ; Hdr: 1 scr, mode $0F, sprite GFX 9, music 4, tileset 5; obj $33, Y=C X=1, size $03
$07:8008                .db $6E,$34,$02,$70,$30,$01,$14,$00 ; obj $33, Y=E X=4, size $02; obj $33, Y=0 X=0, size $01; ext $82 Big bush 1 Y=4 X=0
$07:8010                .db $82,$79,$20,$1F,$0B,$06,$80,$FF ; obj $32, Y=9 X=0, size $1F; ext $80 Ghost house entrance Y=B X=6; End of list
$07:8018                .db $C0,$6F,$49,$04,$05,$FF ; Hdr: 1 scr, mode $0F, sprite GFX 9, music 4, tileset 5; End of list

$07:801E                .db $20,$4E,$39,$0B,$01,$14,$00,$82 ; Hdr: 1 scr, mode $0E, sprite GFX 9, music 3, tileset 1; ext $82 Big bush 1 Y=4 X=0
$07:8026                .db $79,$B0,$1F,$0B,$07,$84,$FF ; obj $3B, Y=9 X=0, size $1F; ext $84 Castle entrance Y=B X=7; End of list

$07:802D                .db $20,$00,$09,$00,$04,$0E,$00,$85 ; Hdr: 1 scr, mode $00, sprite GFX 9, music 0, tileset 4; ext $85 Yoshi's house Y=E X=0

$07:805E                .db $19,$FF,$C1,$61,$47,$04,$85,$6C ; End of list; Hdr: 2 scr, mode $01, sprite GFX 7, music 4, tileset 5; obj $33, Y=C X=5, size $02
$07:8066                .db $35,$02,$6E,$39,$01,$0B,$00,$49 ; obj $33, Y=E X=9, size $01; ext $49 Ghost house exit Y=B X=0
$07:806E                .db $30,$5D,$81,$78,$20,$2F,$EC,$31 ; obj $15, Y=0 X=D, size $81; obj $32, Y=8 X=0, size $2F; new scr obj $33, Y=C X=1, size $02

$07:807E                .db $20,$2F,$FF,$C1,$61,$47,$04,$85 ; End of list; Hdr: 2 scr, mode $01, sprite GFX 7, music 4, tileset 5
$07:8086                .db $14,$06,$83,$13,$0C,$82,$93,$06 ; ext $83 Big bush 2 Y=4 X=6; ext $82 Big bush 1 Y=3 X=C; new scr ext $82 Big bush 1 Y=3 X=6
$07:808E                .db $82,$FF                 ; End of list

$07:8090                .db $20,$6E,$39,$0B,$01,$14,$00,$82 ; Hdr: 1 scr, mode $0E, sprite GFX 9, music 3, tileset 1; ext $82 Big bush 1 Y=4 X=0

;;; $8100: Level object streams (bank 07 overflow) (LevelData078100) ;;;
{
;; Overflow Layer 1/Layer 2 level data for 34 levels that did not fit in bank 06. Each stream = 5-byte primary header,
;; then 3-byte objects (byte1 = N BB H YYYY: new-screen flag, object# bits 5-6, hi-coord flag, Y; byte2 = NNNN XXXX; byte3 = size/settings),
;; ended by $FF. Extended objects (object# 0) use byte3 as the extended number; ext 0 (screen exit) is 4 bytes: the 4th byte is the destination.
;; Line comments decode each header and object: "scr" counts new-screen flags (ext 1 sets it), "hi" = Y/X high-coord bit. Followed by $FF padding.
$07:8100                .db $CE,$60,$98,$A4,$13,$24,$23,$C4 ; [L1 of level $01D] Hdr: 15 scr, mode $00, tileset 3 (Underground 1), music $06, timer 300, spr GFX 8; Slopes: scr 0 x3 y4 sz$C4

;; Layer-1 level streams for levels whose data did not fit in bank 06 (pointed to by Layer1Ptrs $05E000). 35 streams, $07A600-$07C225,
;; each: 5-byte primary header, then 3-byte objects (4 bytes for screen exit), terminated by $FF. $07C226-$07C2FF is $FF padding.
;; Object bytes: [+scr/Y/page-half/obj# high bits], [obj# low nibble / X], [size or extended object number]. Obj 00 = extended object.
;; Extended 00 = screen exit (extra 4th byte = destination level), 01 = screen jump. Row comments decode objects that start on that row.

;;; $C300: Sprite lists, goal bonus digits (LevelDataSpr07C300) ;;;
{
;; Level sprite lists ($07C300-$07E76D): one list per level, pointed to by Ptrs05EC00 (indexed by level number). 12 lists are unreferenced.
;; List format: 1 header byte (bits 0-5 sprite memory setting [$1692], bits 6-7 buoyancy [$190E]), then 3-byte records, then FF.
;; Record: YYYYEEsy (Y row bits 4-7, y = Y bit 4, EE = extra bits, s = screen bit 4), XXXXSSSS (X column, screen low nibble), sprite number.
;; Sorted by screen. Numbers C9-CA shooters, CB-D9 generators, DA-E0 special spawns, E1-E6 clusters, E7+ scroll commands.
;; Row comments decode the records starting in each row: sprite number and name, X column, Y row, screen; mem/buoy = header.
;; $07E76E-$07EFFF is FF padding. $07F000: OAM index table per sprite memory setting (read at $0180D2), $F0B4 gives row bases.
;; $07F0C8-$07F1A9: dot-matrix digit glyphs (X and Y offsets, FF-terminated) for the goal bonus stars number; $F1AA bonus values.
;; $07F1CA: draws that number for the goal tape sprite (called at $01C17A). Parameters: X = sprite index; $1594,X = goal bar step; $1540,X = timer.
$07:C300                .db $03,$00,$80,$F4,$51,$60,$5E,$41 ; unused list: hdr mem=3 buoy=0; F4 cmd0 S00; 5E X6 Y15 S00; 08 XA Y14 S03

$07:F0E8                .db $08,$10,$10,$00,$08,$10,$FF,$00 ; X offsets of dots, digit 4 starts; FF ends a digit
$07:F0F0                .db $00,$10,$00,$10,$00,$08,$10,$10 ; X offsets of dots, digit 4 (cont.)
$07:F0F8                .db $FF,$00,$08,$10,$00,$00,$08,$10 ; X offsets of dots, digit 5 starts; FF ends a digit
$07:F100                .db $10,$00,$08,$10,$FF,$08,$10,$00 ; X offsets of dots, digit 6 starts; FF ends a digit
$07:F108                .db $00,$08,$10,$00,$10,$00,$08,$10 ; X offsets of dots, digit 6 (cont.)
$07:F110                .db $FF,$00,$08,$10,$10,$0C,$08,$08 ; X offsets of dots, digit 7 starts; FF ends a digit
$07:F118                .db $FF,$00,$08,$10,$00,$10,$00,$08 ; X offsets of dots, digit 8 starts; FF ends a digit
$07:F120                .db $10,$00,$10,$00,$08,$10,$FF,$00 ; X offsets of dots, digit 9 starts; FF ends a digit
$07:F128                .db $08,$10,$00,$10,$00,$08,$10,$10 ; X offsets of dots, digit 9 (cont.)
$07:F130                .db $00,$08,$10,$FF         ; X offsets of dots, digit 9 (cont.); FF ends a digit

$07:F134                .db $00,$00,$00,$08,$08,$10,$10,$18 ; Y offsets of dots, digit 0 starts
$07:F13C                .db $18,$20,$20,$20,$FF,$00,$08,$10 ; Y offsets of dots, digit 1 starts; FF ends a digit
$07:F144                .db $18,$20,$FF,$00,$00,$00,$08,$10 ; Y offsets of dots, digit 2 starts; FF ends a digit
$07:F14C                .db $18,$20,$20,$20,$FF,$00,$00,$08 ; Y offsets of dots, digit 3 starts; FF ends a digit
$07:F154                .db $10,$10,$18,$20,$20,$20,$FF,$00 ; Y offsets of dots, digit 4 starts; FF ends a digit
$07:F15C                .db $08,$08,$10,$10,$18,$18,$18,$20 ; Y offsets of dots, digit 4 (cont.)
$07:F164                .db $FF,$00,$00,$00,$08,$10,$10,$10 ; Y offsets of dots, digit 5 starts; FF ends a digit
$07:F16C                .db $18,$20,$20,$20,$FF,$00,$00,$08 ; Y offsets of dots, digit 6 starts; FF ends a digit
$07:F174                .db $10,$10,$10,$18,$18,$20,$20,$20 ; Y offsets of dots, digit 6 (cont.)
$07:F17C                .db $FF,$00,$00,$00,$08,$10,$18,$20 ; Y offsets of dots, digit 7 starts; FF ends a digit
$07:F184                .db $FF,$00,$00,$00,$08,$08,$10,$10 ; Y offsets of dots, digit 8 starts; FF ends a digit
$07:F18C                .db $10,$18,$18,$20,$20,$20,$FF,$00 ; Y offsets of dots, digit 9 starts; FF ends a digit
$07:F194                .db $00,$00,$08,$08,$10,$10,$10,$18 ; Y offsets of dots, digit 9 (cont.)
$07:F19C                .db $20,$20,$20,$FF         ; Y offsets of dots, digit 9 (cont.); FF ends a digit

$07:F1A0                .db $00,$0D,$13,$1D,$27,$31,$3D,$49 ; Start offset of each digit 0-7 in $F0C8/F134
$07:F1A8                .db $51,$5F                 ; Start offset of digits 8-9

$07:F1AA                .db $01,$02,$03,$04,$05,$06,$07,$08 ; Bonus star count (BCD) for steps 00-07: 01,02,03,04,05,06,07,08
$07:F1B2                .db $09,$10,$11,$12,$13,$14,$15,$16 ; Bonus star count (BCD) for steps 08-0F: 09,10,11,12,13,14,15,16
$07:F1BA                .db $17,$18,$19,$20,$21,$22,$23,$24 ; Bonus star count (BCD) for steps 10-17: 17,18,19,20,21,22,23,24
$07:F1C2                .db $25,$26,$27,$28,$29,$30,$40,$50 ; Bonus star count (BCD) for steps 18-1F: 25,26,27,28,29,30,40,50

$07:F1CA  BD 40 15      LDA.W $1540,X               ; A = bonus display timer [$1540,X]
$07:F1CD  85 04         STA $04                     ; $04 = timer
$07:F1CF  64 02         STZ $02                     ; $02 = 0: X shift of tens digit
$07:F1D1  BD 94 15      LDA.W $1594,X               ; \
$07:F1D4  4A            LSR                          ; |
$07:F1D5  4A            LSR                          ; |
$07:F1D6  AA            TAX                          ; } X = [$1594,X] / 4: step of goal bar hit height
$07:F1D7  BF AA F1 07   LDA.L $F1AA,X         ; A = bonus star count in BCD (01-50)
$07:F1DB  48            PHA                          ; Save the count
$07:F1DC  4A            LSR                          ; \
$07:F1DD  4A            LSR                          ; |
$07:F1DE  4A            LSR                          ; |
$07:F1DF  4A            LSR                          ; |
$07:F1E0  AA            TAX                          ; } X = tens digit
$07:F1E1  F0 0A         BEQ $F1ED             ; Skip the tens digit if it is 0
$07:F1E3  BF A0 F1 07   LDA.L $F1A0,X         ; X = start offset of that digit glyph ($F1A0)
$07:F1E7  AA            TAX                          ; Index into the glyph tables
$07:F1E8  A0 20         LDY.B #$20                  ; Y = OAM index $20 for the tens digit dots
$07:F1EA  20 00 F2      JSR.W $F200           ; Draw tens digit dots
$07:F1ED  68            PLA                          ; Restore the count
$07:F1EE  29 0F         AND.B #$0F                  ; \
$07:F1F0  AA            TAX                          ; } X = units digit
$07:F1F1  BF A0 F1 07   LDA.L $F1A0,X         ; A = start offset of that digit glyph
$07:F1F5  AA            TAX                          ; Index into the glyph tables
$07:F1F6  A9 20         LDA.B #$20                  ; \
$07:F1F8  85 02         STA $02                     ; } $02 = $20: units digit is drawn 32 pixels to the right
$07:F1FA  A0 54         LDY.B #$54                  ; Y = OAM index $54 for the units digit dots
$07:F1FC  20 00 F2      JSR.W $F200           ; Draw units digit dots
$07:F1FF  6B            RTL                          ; Return

$07:F200  BF C8 F0 07   LDA.L $F0C8,X         ; A = dot X offset from glyph table
$07:F204  30 44         BMI $F24A             ; FF terminator: digit finished
$07:F206  18            CLC                          ; \
$07:F207  69 64         ADC.B #$64                  ; |
$07:F209  18            CLC                          ; |
$07:F20A  65 02         ADC $02                     ; |
$07:F20C  99 00 02      STA.W OAM_ExtendedDispX,Y   ; } OAM X = offset + $64 + digit X shift [$02]
$07:F20F  BF 34 F1 07   LDA.L $F134,X         ; A = dot Y offset from glyph table
$07:F213  18            CLC                          ; \
$07:F214  69 40         ADC.B #$40                  ; |
$07:F216  99 01 02      STA.W OAM_ExtendedDispY,Y   ; } OAM Y = offset + $40
$07:F219  A9 EF         LDA.B #$EF                  ; A = tile $EF (default dot tile)
$07:F21B  DA            PHX                          ; Save glyph index
$07:F21C  A6 04         LDX $04                     ; X = bonus display timer [$04]
$07:F21E  E0 10         CPX.B #$10                  ; \
$07:F220  B0 08         BCS $F22A             ; } Timer >= $10: keep tile $EF
$07:F222  8A            TXA                          ; \
$07:F223  4A            LSR                          ; |
$07:F224  4A            LSR                          ; |
$07:F225  AA            TAX                          ; |
$07:F226  BF 4E F2 07   LDA.L $F24E,X         ; } Last 16 frames: tile = $F24E[timer / 4]
$07:F22A  99 02 02      STA.W OAM_ExtendedTile,Y    ; Set tile number
$07:F22D  FA            PLX                          ; Restore glyph index
$07:F22E  A5 13         LDA RAM_FrameCounter        ; \
$07:F230  4A            LSR                          ; |
$07:F231  29 0E         AND.B #$0E                  ; |
$07:F233  09 30         ORA.B #$30                  ; |
$07:F235  99 03 02      STA.W OAM_ExtendedProp,Y    ; } Property = $30 | frame counter / 2 & $0E (cycling palette)
$07:F238  5A            PHY                          ; \
$07:F239  98            TYA                          ; |
$07:F23A  4A            LSR                          ; |
$07:F23B  4A            LSR                          ; |
$07:F23C  A8            TAY                          ; |
$07:F23D  A9 00         LDA.B #$00                  ; |
$07:F23F  99 20 04      STA.W $0420,Y               ; } OAM size table: small tile, X bit 8 = 0
$07:F242  7A            PLY                          ; Restore OAM index
$07:F243  C8            INY                          ; \
$07:F244  C8            INY                          ; |
$07:F245  C8            INY                          ; |
$07:F246  C8            INY                          ; } Next OAM slot (+4)
$07:F247  E8            INX                          ; Next dot
$07:F248  80 B6         BRA $F200             ; Loop

$07:F24A  AE E9 15      LDX.W $15E9                 ; X = current sprite index [$15E9]
$07:F24D  60            RTS                          ; Return


}

;;; $F24E..F24E: Table: effect tile numbers ;;;
{
$07:F24E                .db $66,$66,$6E,$FF         ; Tile numbers $66,$66,$6E, then $FF terminator; indexed by [X]/4 (see $F226)

}

;;; $F252: Set score value from height and give points ;;;
{
;; Called from bank 01 ($01:C11F) after a sprite stores a height value in [$1594,X].
;; Looks up [$1594,X]/4 in the score-value table at $07F1AA, stores it in $1900 and, if the value is $50, awards points ($0A).
;; Purpose is not fully certain; the exact use of $1900 was not traced.
;; Called by: $01:C11F (Goal tape movement, check and draw)
$07:F252  DA            PHX                          ; Save sprite index
$07:F253  BD 94 15      LDA.W $1594,X               ; \
$07:F256  4A            LSR                          ; |
$07:F257  4A            LSR                          ; |
$07:F258  AA            TAX                          ; / Index = [$1594,X] / 4
$07:F259  BF AA F1 07   LDA.L $F1AA,X         ; A = entry from the shared value table at $07F1AA (starts 01,02,03... = score sprite numbers)
$07:F25D  8D 00 19      STA.W $1900                 ; $1900 = that value (read by the score/points handling)
$07:F260  FA            PLX                          ; Restore sprite index
$07:F261  C9 50         CMP.B #$50                  ; Was the value $50?
$07:F263  D0 06         BNE $F26B            ; If not, done
$07:F265  A9 0A         LDA.B #$0A                  ; A = $0A (score sprite number)
$07:F267  22 E5 AC 02   JSL.L GivePoints            ; Give points for that score value
$07:F26B  6B            RTL                          ; Return


}

;;; $F26C: Table: sprite tweaker $1656 by sprite number (Sprite1656Vals) ;;;

;; Per-sprite object interaction / clipping property flags.

$07:F27C                .db $10,$10,$14,$00,$00,$00,$00,$10 ; Sprites 10-17

$07:F28C                .db $82,$00,$13,$13,$13,$13,$01,$00 ; Sprites 20-27
$07:F294                .db $00,$00,$81,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00 ; Sprites 28-2F
$07:F29C                .db $00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$05,$80,$00 ; Sprites 30-37

$07:F314                .db $00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00 ; Sprites A8-AF

$07:F324                .db $00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$70,$10,$0E ; Sprites B8-BF

;;; $F3FE: Table: sprite tweaker $166E by sprite number (Sprite166EVals) ;;;

;; also loaded into RAM_SpritePal by LoadSpriteTables; upper bits are interaction flags (fireball, cape, water splash...).
$07:F3FE                .db $0A,$08,$06,$04,$0A,$08,$06,$04 ; Sprites 00-07

;;; $F590: Table: sprite tweaker $1686 by sprite number (Sprite1686Vals) ;;;

$07:F5E8                .db $A9,$A9,$A9,$A9,$A9,$A9,$A9,$A9 ; Sprites 58-5F

$07:F5F8                .db $3D,$29,$19,$29,$29,$59,$59,$18 ; Sprites 68-6F
$07:F600                .db $18,$10,$10,$50,$28,$28,$28,$28 ; Sprites 70-77
$07:F608                .db $08,$29,$29,$39,$39,$29,$28,$28 ; Sprites 78-7F

$07:F618                .db $29,$29,$29,$29,$29,$29,$29,$29 ; Sprites 88-8F
$07:F620                .db $11,$11,$11,$11,$11,$11,$11,$11 ; Sprites 90-97
$07:F628                .db $11,$10,$11,$01,$39,$10,$19,$19 ; Sprites 98-9F
$07:F630                .db $19,$19,$01,$29,$98,$14,$14,$10 ; Sprites A0-A7
$07:F638                .db $18,$18,$18,$00,$19,$19,$19,$19 ; Sprites A8-AF
$07:F640                .db $19,$1D,$1D,$19,$19,$18,$18,$19 ; Sprites B0-B7
$07:F648                .db $19,$19,$1D,$19,$18,$00,$10,$00 ; Sprites B8-BF

$07:F658                .db $19                     ; Sprites C8-C8

;;; $F659: Table: sprite tweaker $190F by sprite number (Sprite190FVals) ;;;
{
;; One byte per sprite number $00-$C8; copied into RAM_Tweaker190F,X. Misc flags (e.g. wall/ledge handling, cape and silver-coin behaviour).
$07:F659                .db $00,$00,$00,$00,$A0,$A0,$A0,$A0 ; Sprites 00-07
$07:F661                .db $B0,$B0,$B0,$B0,$A0,$80,$44,$80 ; Sprites 08-0F
$07:F669                .db $80,$80,$20,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00 ; Sprites 10-17
$07:F671                .db $00,$20,$20,$00,$00,$00,$60,$20 ; Sprites 18-1F

$07:F681                .db $00,$44,$20,$04,$44,$C4,$00,$C4 ; Sprites 28-2F
$07:F689                .db $24,$24,$24,$04,$04,$46,$04,$04 ; Sprites 30-37
$07:F691                .db $04,$04,$04,$04,$04,$00,$C4,$00 ; Sprites 38-3F
$07:F699                .db $00,$05,$05,$05,$04,$44,$48,$00 ; Sprites 40-47
$07:F6A1                .db $00,$40,$40,$40,$04,$00,$00,$00 ; Sprites 48-4F
$07:F6A9                .db $00,$00,$64,$C4,$64,$45,$65,$45 ; Sprites 50-57
$07:F6B1                .db $65,$45,$45,$45,$45,$65,$65,$45 ; Sprites 58-5F
$07:F6B9                .db $45,$45,$45,$45,$45,$45,$45,$05 ; Sprites 60-67
$07:F6C1                .db $05,$44,$44,$44,$44,$46,$00,$00 ; Sprites 68-6F

$07:F6D1                .db $40,$40,$40,$42,$42,$40,$40,$40 ; Sprites 78-7F

$07:F6E1                .db $40,$40,$40,$40,$40,$40,$40,$01 ; Sprites 88-8F
$07:F6E9                .db $00,$48,$48,$48,$48,$48,$48,$48 ; Sprites 90-97
$07:F6F1                .db $48,$00,$40,$40,$40,$00,$04,$04 ; Sprites 98-9F

$07:F701                .db $00,$40,$00,$00,$40,$40,$00,$00 ; Sprites A8-AF
$07:F709                .db $00,$40,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$41 ; Sprites B0-B7

$07:F719                .db $47,$45,$00,$00,$41,$41,$FF,$40 ; Sprites C0-C7
$07:F721                .db $40                     ; Sprites C8-C8

;;; $F722: Clear per-sprite state tables (ZeroSpriteTables) ;;;
{
;; Zeroes the sprite state, timers, speeds, direction and miscellaneous per-slot tables for sprite slot X, then sets the offscreen-horizontal flag to 1.
;; Parameters: X = sprite slot. Does not clear sprite number, position or status.
;; Called by: $07:F7D2 (Initialise sprite slot tables (InitSpriteTables))
$07:F722  9E 4A 16      STZ.W $164A,X               ; $164A,X = 0
$07:F725  9E 32 16      STZ.W RAM_SprBehindScrn,X   ; Sprite behind scenery flag = 0
$07:F728  74 C2         STZ RAM_SpriteState,X       ; Sprite state = 0
$07:F72A  9E 1C 15      STZ.W $151C,X               ; $151C,X = 0
$07:F72D  9E 28 15      STZ.W $1528,X               ; $1528,X = 0
$07:F730  9E 34 15      STZ.W $1534,X               ; $1534,X = 0
$07:F733  9E 7C 15      STZ.W RAM_SpriteDir,X       ; Sprite direction = 0
$07:F736  9E 88 15      STZ.W RAM_SprObjStatus,X    ; Sprite object-contact status = 0
$07:F739  9E C4 15      STZ.W $15C4,X               ; $15C4,X = 0
$07:F73C  9E 02 16      STZ.W $1602,X               ; $1602,X = 0 (animation frame)
$07:F73F  9E 40 15      STZ.W $1540,X               ; $1540,X = 0 (timer)
$07:F742  9E 4C 15      STZ.W RAM_DisableInter,X    ; Disable-interaction timer = 0
$07:F745  9E 58 15      STZ.W $1558,X               ; $1558,X = 0 (timer)
$07:F748  9E 64 15      STZ.W $1564,X               ; $1564,X = 0 (timer)
$07:F74B  9E E2 1F      STZ.W $1FE2,X               ; $1FE2,X = 0
$07:F74E  9E 26 16      STZ.W $1626,X               ; $1626,X = 0
$07:F751  9E 70 15      STZ.W $1570,X               ; $1570,X = 0
$07:F754  74 B6         STZ RAM_SpriteSpeedX,X      ; Sprite X speed = 0
$07:F756  9E F8 14      STZ.W $14F8,X               ; Sprite X fraction bits = 0
$07:F759  74 AA         STZ RAM_SpriteSpeedY,X      ; Sprite Y speed = 0
$07:F75B  9E EC 14      STZ.W $14EC,X               ; Sprite Y fraction bits = 0
$07:F75E  9E DC 15      STZ.W $15DC,X               ; $15DC,X = 0
$07:F761  9E D0 15      STZ.W $15D0,X               ; $15D0,X = 0 (being eaten / on Yoshi's tongue flag)
$07:F764  9E 3E 16      STZ.W $163E,X               ; $163E,X = 0 (timer)
$07:F767  9E 56 16      STZ.W RAM_Tweaker1656,X     ; Tweaker $1656,X = 0 (overwritten later by LoadTweakerBytes)
$07:F76A  9E 62 16      STZ.W RAM_Tweaker1662,X     ; Tweaker $1662,X = 0
$07:F76D  9E 6E 16      STZ.W RAM_Tweaker166E,X     ; Tweaker $166E,X = 0
$07:F770  9E 7A 16      STZ.W RAM_Tweaker167A,X     ; Tweaker $167A,X = 0
$07:F773  9E 86 16      STZ.W RAM_Tweaker1686,X     ; Tweaker $1686,X = 0
$07:F776  9E 7B 18      STZ.W $187B,X               ; $187B,X = 0
$07:F779  9E 0E 16      STZ.W $160E,X               ; $160E,X = 0
$07:F77C  9E 94 15      STZ.W $1594,X               ; $1594,X = 0
$07:F77F  9E 04 15      STZ.W $1504,X               ; $1504,X = 0
$07:F782  9E D6 1F      STZ.W $1FD6,X               ; $1FD6,X = 0
$07:F785  A9 01         LDA.B #$01                  ; A = 1
$07:F787  9D A0 15      STA.W RAM_OffscreenHorz,X   ; Offscreen horizontal flag = 1 (treated as off screen until processed)
$07:F78A  6B            RTL                          ; Return

}

;;; $F78B: Load sprite palette and tweaker bytes (LoadSpriteTables) ;;;
{
;; Sets RAM_SpritePal,X from the low nibble of the sprite's $166E tweaker value, then calls LoadTweakerBytes.
;; Parameters: X = sprite slot (sprite number already in RAM_SpriteNum,X). Preserves X and Y.
;; Called by: $01:9675 (Koopa spawn table; Bob-omb and shell timers (SpriteKoopasSpawn)), $01:A9A4 (Mario-sprite contact: stomp, kick, hurt (ReturnNoContact2)), $01:C335 (Changing item (cycling power-up) main (ChangingItem)), $01:C340 (Changing item (cycling power-up) main (ChangingItem)), $01:D67C (Parachute sprites: draw and physics), $01:F2F9 (Spit sound and sprite change), $02:949E (Bounce block and cape hit sprites), $02:E0CD (Jumping Piranha Plant main (JumpingPiranhaMain)), $03:8197 (Big Boo boss: remaining states and helpers), $07:F7D6 (Initialise sprite slot tables (InitSpriteTables))
$07:F78B  5A            PHY                          ; Save Y
$07:F78C  DA            PHX                          ; Save sprite index
$07:F78D  B5 9E         LDA RAM_SpriteNum,X         ; A = sprite number
$07:F78F  AA            TAX                          ; X = sprite number
$07:F790  BF FE F3 07   LDA.L Sprite166EVals,X      ; A = tweaker byte $166E for that sprite type
$07:F794  29 0F         AND.B #$0F                  ; Keep low nibble: palette bits and graphics page bit
$07:F796  FA            PLX                          ; Restore sprite index
$07:F797  9D F6 15      STA.W RAM_SpritePal,X       ; Sprite palette/page = [$166E] & $0F
$07:F79A  22 A0 F7 07   JSL.L LoadTweakerBytes      ; Copy all tweaker bytes into the slot
$07:F79E  7A            PLY                          ; Restore Y
$07:F79F  6B            RTL                          ; Return

}

;;; $F7A0: Copy tweaker bytes into sprite slot (LoadTweakerBytes) ;;;
{
;; Copies the six tweaker bytes of the slot's sprite number from the Sprite*Vals tables into $1656/$1662/$166E/$167A/$1686/$190F,X.
;; Parameters: X = sprite slot. Preserves X and Y.
;; Called by: $01:FB36 (Iggy/Larry: tilting-platform Koopaling (PlatformKoopaKids)), $07:F79A (Load sprite palette and tweaker bytes (LoadSpriteTables))
$07:F7A0  5A            PHY                          ; Save Y
$07:F7A1  DA            PHX                          ; Save X
$07:F7A2  9B            TXY                          ; Y = sprite slot
$07:F7A3  B6 9E         LDX RAM_SpriteNum,Y         ; X = sprite number of slot Y
$07:F7A5  BF 6C F2 07   LDA.L Sprite1656Vals,X      ; \
$07:F7A9  99 56 16      STA.W RAM_Tweaker1656,Y     ; / Tweaker $1656,Y = Sprite1656Vals[sprite number]
$07:F7AC  BF 35 F3 07   LDA.L Sprite1662Vals,X      ; \
$07:F7B0  99 62 16      STA.W RAM_Tweaker1662,Y     ; / Tweaker $1662,Y = Sprite1662Vals[sprite number]
$07:F7B3  BF FE F3 07   LDA.L Sprite166EVals,X      ; \
$07:F7B7  99 6E 16      STA.W RAM_Tweaker166E,Y     ; / Tweaker $166E,Y = Sprite166EVals[sprite number]
$07:F7BA  BF C7 F4 07   LDA.L Sprite167AVals,X      ; \
$07:F7BE  99 7A 16      STA.W RAM_Tweaker167A,Y     ; / Tweaker $167A,Y = Sprite167AVals[sprite number]
$07:F7C1  BF 90 F5 07   LDA.L Sprite1686Vals,X      ; \
$07:F7C5  99 86 16      STA.W RAM_Tweaker1686,Y     ; / Tweaker $1686,Y = Sprite1686Vals[sprite number]
$07:F7C8  BF 59 F6 07   LDA.L Sprite190FVals,X      ; \
$07:F7CC  99 0F 19      STA.W RAM_Tweaker190F,Y     ; / Tweaker $190F,Y = Sprite190FVals[sprite number]
$07:F7CF  FA            PLX                          ; Restore X
$07:F7D0  7A            PLY                          ; Restore Y
$07:F7D1  6B            RTL                          ; Return

}

;;; $F7D2: Initialise sprite slot tables (InitSpriteTables) ;;;
{
;; Runs ZeroSpriteTables then LoadSpriteTables for sprite slot X.
;; Called by: $00:FA75 (Spawn Flat Palace Switch (FlatPalaceSwitch)), $00:FB60 (Level end: convert sprite to bonus powerup (LvlEndStoreSpr)), $00:FCBC (Level-end coin sprite and helpers (LvlEndSprCoinsRt)), $01:84C6 (Init Lakitu and spawn cloud (InitLakitu)), $01:8C34 (Spiny egg main routine (SpinyEgg)), $01:96F6 (Spawn shelless Koopa from shell (GeneralResetSpr)), $01:9798 (Spawn moving coin from Koopa (SpawnMovingCoin)), $01:A2DC (Baby Yoshi: stunned/carried logic and draw (StunBabyYoshi)), $01:BC97 (Spawn sprite from the magic (StoreSpriteNum)), $01:BF53 (Magikoopa visible state, fade, goal tape init), $01:D0A4 (Spawn boss fireball), $01:DDD9 (Sprite 82: Init bonus game (InitBonusGame)) ...
$07:F7D2  22 22 F7 07   JSL.L ZeroSpriteTables      ; Clear the per-sprite RAM tables of slot X
$07:F7D6  22 8B F7 07   JSL.L LoadSpriteTables      ; Load palette and tweaker bytes for slot X
$07:F7DA  6B            RTL                          ; Return

;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;
;;This is the sine/cosine table, most of the values are often below
;;#$0100, but because of this, it has to be 16-bit values. Most
;;likely that the values were rounded down (or truncated) when
;;converting real world sine/cosine result to SMW. Usage:
;;
;; <A = the angle value, 16-bit, from #$0000-#$00FF>  ;>Angle (0-255 smw degrees, covering half the circle).
;; ASL                                                ;>Multiply by 2 because values are 2 bytes large.
;; AND.W #$01FF                                       ;>modulo by 512 (so that it starts at the beginning on the 2nd half circle)
;; TAX                                                ;>Transfer to index
;; LDA $07F7DB,x                                      ;>Load sine/cosine values from table.
;;
;; Or:
;;
;; <A is currently 16-bit>
;; AND #$00FF                                 ;>Modulo by 256
;; ASL                                        ;>Multiply by 2 (table values = 2 bytes each)
;; TAX                                        ;>Transfer into index
;; LDA $07F7DB,x                              ;>Load sine/cosine values from table.
;;
;;Note that this only covers half a circle. Due to a lack of
;;fractional values, the output is multiplied by #$0100, which
;;means the Y in [sin(X) = Y/256] (numbers in formula are all
;;decimal).
;;
;;Real world and smw degree conversion:
;;- RealWorldDegrees = SMWDegrees*360/512
;;- SMWDegrees = RealWorldDegrees*512/360
;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;
}

;;; $F7DB: Sine table and line-guide path data (CircleCoords) ;;;
{
;; $07F7DB-$07F9DA: 256 16-bit sine/cosine values (sin x 256) for angles 0-255 over half a circle; index with [angle]*2.
;; $07F9DB-$07FBF2: 32 packed paths (probably 4-bit X/Y positions) for line-guided sprites; pointers in the low/high tables at $07FBF3/$07FC13.
;; $07FC33-$07FC8F: speed tables and routine that spawns four spin-jump stars (extended sprite $10) at the current sprite.
$07:F7DB                dw $0000,$0003,$0006,$0009  ; Sine x256 for angles 00-03
                    dw $000C,$000F,$0012,$0015       ;>$07F7E3
                    dw $0019,$001C,$001F,$0022       ;>$07F7EB
                    dw $0025,$0028,$002B,$002E       ;>$07F7F3
                    dw $0031,$0035,$0038,$003B       ;>$07F7FB
                    dw $003E,$0041,$0044,$0047       ;>$07F803
                    dw $004A,$004D,$0050,$0053       ;>$07F80B
                    dw $0056,$0059,$005C,$005F       ;>$07F813
                    dw $0061,$0064,$0067,$006A       ;>$07F81B
                    dw $006D,$0070,$0073,$0075       ;>$07F823
                    dw $0078,$007B,$007E,$0080       ;>$07F82B
                    dw $0083,$0086,$0088,$008B       ;>$07F833
                    dw $008E,$0090,$0093,$0095       ;>$07F83B
                    dw $0098,$009B,$009D,$009F       ;>$07F843
                    dw $00A2,$00A4,$00A7,$00A9       ;>$07F84B
                    dw $00AB,$00AE,$00B0,$00B2       ;>$07F853
                    dw $00B5,$00B7,$00B9,$00BB       ;>$07F85B
                    dw $00BD,$00BF,$00C1,$00C3       ;>$07F863
                    dw $00C5,$00C7,$00C9,$00CB       ;>$07F86B
                    dw $00CD,$00CF,$00D1,$00D3       ;>$07F873
                    dw $00D4,$00D6,$00D8,$00D9       ;>$07F87B
                    dw $00DB,$00DD,$00DE,$00E0       ;>$07F883
                    dw $00E1,$00E3,$00E4,$00E6       ;>$07F88B
                    dw $00E7,$00E8,$00EA,$00EB       ;>$07F893
                    dw $00EC,$00ED,$00EE,$00EF       ;>$07F89B
                    dw $00F1,$00F2,$00F3,$00F4       ;>$07F8A3
                    dw $00F4,$00F5,$00F6,$00F7       ;>$07F8AB
                    dw $00F8,$00F9,$00F9,$00FA       ;>$07F8B3
                    dw $00FB,$00FB,$00FC,$00FC       ;>$07F8BB
                    dw $00FD,$00FD,$00FE,$00FE       ;>$07F8C3
                    dw $00FE,$00FF,$00FF,$00FF       ;>$07F8CB
                    dw $00FF,$00FF,$00FF,$00FF       ;>$07F8D3
                    dw $0100,$00FF,$00FF,$00FF       ;>$07F8DB
                    dw $00FF,$00FF,$00FF,$00FF       ;>$07F8E3
                    dw $00FE,$00FE,$00FE,$00FD       ;>$07F8EB
                    dw $00FD,$00FC,$00FC,$00FB       ;>$07F8F3
                    dw $00FB,$00FA,$00F9,$00F9       ;>$07F8FB
                    dw $00F8,$00F7,$00F6,$00F5       ;>$07F903
                    dw $00F4,$00F4,$00F3,$00F2       ;>$07F90B
                    dw $00F1,$00EF,$00EE,$00ED       ;>$07F913
                    dw $00EC,$00EB,$00EA,$00E8       ;>$07F91B
                    dw $00E7,$00E6,$00E4,$00E3       ;>$07F923
                    dw $00E1,$00E0,$00DE,$00DD       ;>$07F92B
                    dw $00DB,$00D9,$00D8,$00D6       ;>$07F933
                    dw $00D4,$00D3,$00D1,$00CF       ;>$07F93B
                    dw $00CD,$00CB,$00C9,$00C7       ;>$07F943
                    dw $00C5,$00C3,$00C1,$00BF       ;>$07F94B
                    dw $00BD,$00BB,$00B9,$00B7       ;>$07F953
                    dw $00B5,$00B2,$00B0,$00AE       ;>$07F95B
                    dw $00AB,$00A9,$00A7,$00A4       ;>$07F963
                    dw $00A2,$009F,$009D,$009B       ;>$07F96B
                    dw $0098,$0095,$0093,$0090       ;>$07F973
                    dw $008E,$008B,$0088,$0086       ;>$07F97B
                    dw $0083,$0080,$007E,$007B       ;>$07F983
                    dw $0078,$0075,$0073,$0070       ;>$07F98B
                    dw $006D,$006A,$0067,$0064       ;>$07F993
                    dw $0061,$005F,$005C,$0059       ;>$07F99B
                    dw $0056,$0053,$0050,$004D       ;>$07F9A3
                    dw $004A,$0047,$0044,$0041       ;>$07F9AB
                    dw $003E,$003B,$0038,$0035       ;>$07F9B3
                    dw $0031,$002E,$002B,$0028       ;>$07F9BB
                    dw $0025,$0022,$001F,$001C       ;>$07F9C3
                    dw $0019,$0015,$0012,$000F       ;>$07F9CB
                    dw $000C,$0009,$0006,$0003       ;>$07F9D3
;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;
;;Line-guided sprite speed table
;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;
$07:F9DB                .db $F0,$E0,$D0,$C1,$B1,$A1,$92,$82 ;>$07F9DB
$07:F9E3                .db $73,$64,$55,$46,$37,$28,$29,$1A ;>$07F9E3
$07:F9EB                .db $1B,$1C,$0D,$0E,$0F,$00,$01,$02 ;>$07F9EB
$07:F9F3                .db $13,$14,$15,$26,$27,$38,$49,$5A ;>$07F9F3
$07:F9FB                .db $6B,$7C,$8D,$9D,$AE,$BE,$CE,$DF ;>$07F9FB
$07:FA03                .db $EF,$FF,$00,$10,$20,$31,$41,$51 ;>$07FA03
$07:FA0B                .db $62,$72,$83,$94,$A5,$B6,$C7,$D8 ;>$07FA0B
$07:FA13                .db $D9,$EA,$EB,$EC,$FD,$FD,$FF,$F0 ;>$07FA13
$07:FA1B                .db $F1,$F2,$E3,$E4,$E5,$D6,$D7,$C8 ;>$07FA1B
$07:FA23                .db $B9,$AA,$9B,$8C,$7D,$6D,$5E,$4E ;>$07FA23
$07:FA2B                .db $3E,$2F,$1F,$0F,$F5,$E6,$D6,$C7 ;>$07FA2B
$07:FA33                .db $B8,$A9,$9A,$8B,$7C,$6D,$6E,$5F ;>$07FA33
$07:FA3B                .db $40,$41,$32,$33,$24,$25,$26,$17 ;>$07FA3B
$07:FA43                .db $18,$19,$1A,$0B,$0C,$0D,$0E,$0F ;>$07FA43
$07:FA4B                .db $F0,$E0,$D0,$C0,$B0,$A1,$91,$81 ;>$07FA4B
$07:FA53                .db $71,$62,$52,$42,$33,$23,$14,$04 ;>$07FA53
$07:FA5B                .db $0B,$1B,$2C,$3C,$4D,$5D,$6D,$7E ;>$07FA5B
$07:FA63                .db $8E,$9E,$AE,$BF,$CF,$DF,$EF,$FF ;>$07FA63
$07:FA6B                .db $00,$01,$02,$03,$04,$15,$16,$17 ;>$07FA6B

$07:FA93                .db $B0,$B1,$C2,$C3,$D4,$D5,$D6,$E7 ;>$07FA93
$07:FA9B                .db $E8,$E9,$EA,$FB,$FC,$FD,$FE,$FF ;>$07FA9B

$07:FAEB                .db $69,$5A,$4B,$4B,$3C,$2D,$1E,$1E ;>$07FAEB
$07:FAF3                .db $0F,$00,$11,$22,$22,$33,$44,$55 ;>$07FAF3
$07:FAFB                .db $55,$66,$77,$88,$88,$99,$AA,$BB ;>$07FAFB
$07:FB03                .db $BB,$CC,$DD,$EE,$EE,$FF,$F8,$E8 ;>$07FB03
$07:FB0B                .db $D9,$C9,$BA,$AA,$9B,$8B,$7C,$6C ;>$07FB0B
$07:FB13                .db $5D,$4D,$3E,$2E,$1F,$0F,$00,$10 ;>$07FB13
$07:FB1B                .db $21,$31,$42,$52,$63,$73,$84,$94 ;>$07FB1B
$07:FB23                .db $A5,$B5,$C6,$D6,$E7,$F7,$F0,$E0 ;>$07FB23
$07:FB2B                .db $D1,$C1,$B2,$A2,$93,$83,$74,$64 ;>$07FB2B
$07:FB33                .db $55,$45,$36,$26,$17,$07,$08,$18 ;>$07FB33
$07:FB3B                .db $29,$39,$4A,$5A,$6B,$7B,$8C,$9C ;>$07FB3B
$07:FB43                .db $AD,$BD,$CE,$DE,$EF,$FF,$F0,$F1 ;>$07FB43
$07:FB4B                .db $E2,$E3,$D4,$D5,$C6,$C7,$B8,$B9 ;>$07FB4B
$07:FB53                .db $AA,$AB,$9C,$9D,$8E,$8F,$70,$71 ;>$07FB53
$07:FB5B                .db $62,$63,$54,$55,$46,$47,$38,$39 ;>$07FB5B
$07:FB63                .db $2A,$2B,$1C,$1D,$0E,$0F,$00,$01 ;>$07FB63
$07:FB6B                .db $12,$13,$24,$25,$36,$37,$48,$49 ;>$07FB6B
$07:FB73                .db $5A,$5B,$6C,$6D,$7E,$7F,$80,$81 ;>$07FB73
$07:FB7B                .db $92,$93,$A4,$A5,$B6,$B7,$C8,$C9 ;>$07FB7B
$07:FB83                .db $DA,$DB,$EC,$ED,$FE,$FF,$00,$10 ;>$07FB83
$07:FB8B                .db $20,$30,$40,$50,$60,$70,$80,$90 ;>$07FB8B
$07:FB93                .db $A0,$B0,$C0,$D0,$E0,$F0,$0F,$1F ;>$07FB93
$07:FB9B                .db $2F,$3F,$4F,$5F,$6F,$7F,$8F,$9F ;>$07FB9B
$07:FBA3                .db $AF,$BF,$CF,$DF,$EF,$FF,$00,$01 ;>$07FBA3
$07:FBAB                .db $02,$03,$04,$05,$06,$07,$08,$09 ;>$07FBAB
$07:FBB3                .db $0A,$0B,$0C,$0D,$0E,$0F,$F0,$F1 ;>$07FBB3
$07:FBBB                .db $F2,$F3,$F4,$F5,$F6,$F7,$F8,$F9 ;>$07FBBB
$07:FBC3                .db $FA,$FB,$FC,$FD,$FE,$FF,$F0,$E1 ;>$07FBC3
$07:FBCB                .db $D2,$D2,$C3,$B4,$A5,$A5,$96,$87 ;>$07FBCB
$07:FBD3                .db $78,$78,$69,$5A,$4B,$4B,$3C,$2D ;>$07FBD3
$07:FBDB                .db $1E,$1E,$0F,$00,$11,$22,$22,$33 ;>$07FBDB
$07:FBE3                .db $44,$55,$55,$66,$77,$88,$88,$99 ;>$07FBE3
$07:FBEB                .db $AA,$BB,$BB,$CC,$DD,$EE,$EE,$FF ;>$07FBEB
$07:FBF3                .db $DB,$F0,$05,$1A,$2F,$3B,$4B,$5B ; Line-guide path 0-7: low bytes of path pointers
$07:FBFB                .db $6B,$7B,$87,$93,$A3,$B3,$C3,$D3 ; Line-guide path 8-15: low bytes of path pointers
$07:FC03                .db $DF,$F4,$09,$19,$29,$39,$49,$59 ; Line-guide path 16-23: low bytes of path pointers
$07:FC0B                .db $69,$79,$89,$99,$A9,$B9,$C9,$DE ; Line-guide path 24-31: low bytes of path pointers
$07:FC13                .db $F9,$F9,$FA,$FA,$FA,$FA,$FA,$FA ; Line-guide path 0-7: high bytes of path pointers
$07:FC1B                .db $FA,$FA,$FA,$FA,$FA,$FA,$FA,$FA ; Line-guide path 8-15: high bytes of path pointers
$07:FC23                .db $FA,$FA,$FB,$FB,$FB,$FB,$FB,$FB ; Line-guide path 16-23: high bytes of path pointers
$07:FC2B                .db $FB,$FB,$FB,$FB,$FB,$FB,$FB,$FB ; Line-guide path 24-31: high bytes of path pointers
$07:FC33                .db $E0,$20,$E0,$20         ; Spin star X speeds for stars 0-3: -$20, +$20, -$20, +$20

$07:FC37                .db $F0,$F0,$10,$10         ; Spin star Y speeds for stars 0-3: -$10, -$10, +$10, +$10

$07:FC3B  DA            PHX                          ; Save X
$07:FC3C  A2 03         LDX.B #$03                  ; Star counter X = 3
$07:FC3E  22 47 FC 07   JSL.L $FC47           ; Spawn one spin-jump star
$07:FC42  CA            DEX                          ; Next star
$07:FC43  10 F9         BPL $FC3E             ; Loop for stars 3..0
$07:FC45  FA            PLX                          ; Restore X
$07:FC46  6B            RTL                          ; Return

$07:FC47  A0 07         LDY.B #$07                  ; Y = 7 (highest extended sprite slot)
$07:FC49  B9 0B 17      LDA.W RAM_ExSpriteNum,Y     ; Look for a free extended sprite slot
$07:FC4C  F0 04         BEQ $FC52             ; Free if extended sprite number = 0
$07:FC4E  88            DEY                          ; Next slot down
$07:FC4F  10 F8         BPL $FC49             ; Loop through slots 7..0
$07:FC51  6B            RTL                          ; Return if no free slot

$07:FC52  A9 10         LDA.B #$10                  ; A = extended sprite $10 (spin-jump star)
$07:FC54  99 0B 17      STA.W RAM_ExSpriteNum,Y     ; Create the extended sprite
$07:FC57  DA            PHX                          ; Save star index
$07:FC58  AE E9 15      LDX.W $15E9                 ; X = current sprite index
$07:FC5B  B5 D8         LDA RAM_SpriteYLo,X         ; \
$07:FC5D  18            CLC                          ; |
$07:FC5E  69 04         ADC.B #$04                  ; |
$07:FC60  99 15 17      STA.W RAM_ExSpriteYLo,Y     ; / Star Y low = sprite Y low + 4
$07:FC63  BD D4 14      LDA.W RAM_SpriteYHi,X       ; \
$07:FC66  69 00         ADC.B #$00                  ; |
$07:FC68  99 29 17      STA.W RAM_ExSpriteYHi,Y     ; / Star Y high = sprite Y high + carry
$07:FC6B  B5 E4         LDA RAM_SpriteXLo,X         ; \
$07:FC6D  18            CLC                          ; |
$07:FC6E  69 04         ADC.B #$04                  ; |
$07:FC70  99 1F 17      STA.W RAM_ExSpriteXLo,Y     ; / Star X low = sprite X low + 4
$07:FC73  BD E0 14      LDA.W RAM_SpriteXHi,X       ; \
$07:FC76  69 00         ADC.B #$00                  ; |
$07:FC78  99 33 17      STA.W RAM_ExSpriteXHi,Y     ; / Star X high = sprite X high + carry
$07:FC7B  FA            PLX                          ; Restore star index
$07:FC7C  BF 33 FC 07   LDA.L $FC33,X         ; \
$07:FC80  99 47 17      STA.W RAM_ExSprSpeedX,Y     ; / Star X speed from table by star index
$07:FC83  BF 37 FC 07   LDA.L $FC37,X         ; \
$07:FC87  99 3D 17      STA.W RAM_ExSprSpeedY,Y     ; / Star Y speed from table by star index
$07:FC8A  A9 17         LDA.B #$17                  ; A = $17
$07:FC8C  99 6F 17      STA.W $176F,Y               ; Star lifetime timer = $17 frames
$07:FC8F  6B            RTL                          ; Return


}
